The dst value is the same symbol + offset (A) or the same end (E) in fuzz_transport as the src value in fuzz_grant. The named-symbol rule reads the label as a symbol name and expects the label-named symbol.

## What was measured, and how

- Image src: `bin/fuzz_grant/fuzz_grant.asan` sha256 c2c7559b29bd; symbols from its unstripped twin `unstripped/fuzz_grant/fuzz_grant.asan` sha256 57c26f993615 (`.symtab`). Allocated sections of the twin equal to the stripped image except `.note.gnu.build-id`: yes (checked).
- Image dst: `dgn-transport-pins-port2/stripped/fuzz_transport.asan` sha256 e89be137db17; symbols from its unstripped twin `dgn-transport-pins-port2/unstripped/fuzz_transport.asan` sha256 86efc3a80314 (`.symtab`). Allocated sections of the twin equal to the stripped image except `.note.gnu.build-id`: yes (checked).
- Image bytes, PT_LOAD / PT_TLS / PT_PHDR, section bounds and the asan_globals descriptors: read from the stripped images with pyelftools. IMG_TOP (page-up of the last PT_LOAD end): src 0x5fa000, dst 0x616000; __stop_asan_globals: src 0xa1460, dst 0xa9340; .bss: src 0xa1480, dst 0xa9340; .rodata: src [0x102e0, 0x22c4c), dst [0x102e0, 0x22b4c); program range (LLVMFuzzerTestOneInput .. __wrap_getenv): src [0x806b0, 0x857dc), dst [0x81264, 0x8cfc0); registered globals: src 13 (highest shadow end 0x8000b8dc), dst 40 (0x800b663c).
- Instruction words (SDCP-SIZE only): every `lui` word of .text whose 20-bit immediate is the value / 4096, located by function + offset in both images; only what the word writes to its register is used.
- Text: src = `stage/`, dst = `stage-out/` of the port_core run. Each row's src line and dst line were read; a dst line is the src line + 1 (the added provenance line), unless a patch or a removed row moves it.
- Start-up layer (not relocated; the stand-in of LIB `LIB`, A/): A/AB13.v sha256 1fb1a9636438, A/AF0m.v sha256 5dfdd0e65356, A/AD2j.v sha256 f32f53f83a52. Donor start-up values from fuzz_grant's released lib (`A/AB13.v`, `A/AF0m.v`, `A/AD2j.v`). The run of record changes only the start-up dependent values; re-run this script with its LIB.
- The run's decisions: `check.tsv`, `review.tsv`, `fixed.tsv`, `decimals.tsv`, `patches.log` of the run dir (each row's matching decision is quoted in its detail), the overrides `overrides.tsv` (sha256 50a44ad70d1b), and `zero-shadow-forallb.patch` (c61928f405d4), `cell-lookup.patch` (a4cff1a229c0), `ad1q-shape.patch` (b7a6e350fd47), `imgtop-alias.patch` (a1b73a8107be), `dead-arm-ad1g.patch` (5d1c6411a7be), `bss-zero-range.patch` (6d1acdf0c95f), `g-ACfn.patch` (541e4c4696b9), `g-AD1w.patch` (ed41960ef097), `g-AD1m.patch` (58f8cadc9122), `g-AD1p.patch` (2b55df460405), `g-AD1q.patch` (c353e5cf6157), `g-AD1r.patch` (258880fd6cad), `g-AD2f.patch` (f9d48ede68ec), `afet-cost.patch` (9f5dfea19198) of `EV`.
- Override cross-check: 41 triaged rows carry an override row (same file, donor line, old value); for NOT all of them the dst value equals the override's new value: AD1o:80, AD1o:80, AD1o:83, AD1o:83, AD1o:86, AD1o:90, AD1o:90, AD1o:92, AD1o:94, AD1o:94, AD1o:97, AD1o:97, AD1o:98, AD1o:99, AD1o:99, AD1o:99, AD1o:99, AD25:528, AD25:531, AD25:538, AFem:128, AFem:206, AE2t:63, AE2t:63, AE2u:63, AE2u:63, AE2v:63, AE2v:63, AE2w:63, AE2w:63, AE35:64, AE35:64, AFhn:419, AFix:285, AFix:289, AFjb:284, AFjb:288, AFju:576, AFju:586, AFkm:1305, AFkz:898.

## Reasons

- **P-TRANSPROG** (126 rows; UNPAIRED-PORTED; OK-explained): fuzz_transport's own program functions, written on the dst LLVMFuzzerTestOneInput line of AB1w (A and E rows): each A value is the symtab start, each E value start + size; with the kept rows they cover every function of fuzz_transport's program range [0x81264, 0x8cfc0).
- **N-FMTPTR** (113 rows; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): decimal pointer into a driver format string (a position of the vfprintf walk). Same string, same offset in both images; the string start is checked in the image bytes (NUL before it, bytes equal). The checker classes a decimal that is not a symbol start as non-address, so every moved one is flagged.
- **N-REGCNT** (50 rows; GENERATOR-FILL, NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): count or index in the IMG_REGIONS region list, mapped from the donor's list to the unit's (LIB/A/AD2j.v, the stand-in start-up layer); the run of record recomputes them from the new start-up layer.
- **S-GRANTPROG** (44 rows; UNPAIRED-SOURCE; OK-explained): fuzz_grant's own program functions (AB1w program table): fuzz_transport.asan has none of them (both symtabs read); the port blanked these rows and wrote fuzz_transport's own program functions on the LLVMFuzzerTestOneInput line (P-TRANSPROG). No file of stage-out or LIB uses these names.
- **PATCH-ZS** (27 rows; UNPAIRED-PORTED, UNPAIRED-SOURCE; OK-explained): zero-shadow-forallb.patch: the Tfib_zero_shadow case split becomes a forallb check. Lines the patch deletes (src) or adds (dst) carry literal 8s (shadow granule) and 0s (remainder); each row's line is a deleted / added line of the patch for its file.
- **P-BSSZR** (20 rows; DIFFERENT-SYMBOL, GEOMETRY-CHANGED, KIND-CHANGED, NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): bss-zero-range.patch: A/AD1o.v bss_zero_shadow / bss_shadow_facts and A/AD25.v rs_zero_shadow take one piece of LIB/A/AF0m.v Tfib_zero_shadow each (the donor's one range over the whole .bss after asan_globals is not in this image's list); each value is an end of that piece, the start + 0x20, or a quotient by 8 of them, on a line the patch added. Where an override row wrote the line first, port_core reports it `removed by --patch`.
- **SV-MMAP** (18 rows; SAME-VALUE-DIFFERENT-OBJECT; TOOL-LIMIT): mmap flags word (YA01 node_mmap2g, YA09 mmap_noreserve_b / ff_class / noreplace_core): each value decomposes into MAP_PRIVATE 0x2, MAP_ANONYMOUS 0x20 [, MAP_FIXED 0x10] [, MAP_NORESERVE 0x4000], MAP_FIXED_NOREPLACE 0x100000 with remainder 0. ABI constants; never relocated. The checker reads each as an address inside internal_alloc_placeholder and gives a moved expected value (its FLAGS-NOT-DECOMPOSABLE reading does not reach these positions).
- **P-GHUNK** (10 rows; UNPAIRED-PORTED, UNPAIRED-SOURCE; OK-explained): the G patches (g-ACfn, g-AD1w, g-AD1m, g-AD1p, g-AD1q, g-AD1r, g-AD2f; records/ghunks/GHUNKS-A-rec3.md): a numeral below the image base 0x10000 (no address of either image) on a line a G patch added (the out-window count 0 of `ajournal_gx ... G 0 []`, the bounds of `0 <= a < 2 ^ 64` in the memory-agreement hypothesis), or on a line it deleted whose replacement keeps the value (the same sentence with the G names added).
- **FMTWIN** (10 rows; KIND-CHANGED, NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED, UNRESOLVED; OK-explained): driver format-string shadow window [SH_LO, SH_HI): SH_LO = floor8(0x7fff8000 + fmt/8), SH_HI = floor8(0x7fff8000 + (fmt+len)/8) + 8, each side from its own image's string at `Definition FMT` of the file (bytes read from the image, equal on both sides). The checker names a shadow address only where an object starts in the 8 bytes it covers. 10 of the 10 dst values are override rows of `overrides.tsv` (each equal to its new value).
- **G-6128** (9 rows; GEOMETRY-CHANGED; TOOL-LIMIT): 6128 = kPage 4096 + kSecRz 2048 - kChunkHeaderSize 16 (stage/Reusable/YG00.v): the chunk header offset from the runtime mmap base aMAP (`SOff aMAP 6128`, `hdr = MAP + 6128`), not an image quantity. The checker reads it as the donor distance load2.memhi - .got.lo (0x9e000 - 0x9c810 = 0x17f0, a coincidence) and expects fuzz_transport's 0xfd0, which has no role here.
- **K-CZONE** (9 rows; UNRESOLVED; OK-explained): CZONE bound 0x800b9c00 = kLowShadowBeg 0x7fff7000 + IMG_TOP 0x616000 / 8, the least shadow start of the secondary map that map_facts SM1 (A/AFjm.v) allows, so the CZONE window stays below it (the goal of A/AFju.v CZ_untouched); above the highest shadow end of fuzz_transport's registered globals 0x800b663c; the donor's 0x80050000 is below it. The owner's decision (override rows). 9 of the 9 dst values are override rows of `overrides.tsv` (each equal to its new value).
- **P-LINEPAIR** (4 rows; UNPAIRED-PORTED, UNPAIRED-SOURCE; OK-explained): a numeral on a line no patch changed, in a file a patch changed (afet-cost.patch, A/AFet.v), which the checker leaves unpaired because the patch changed the sentence before it. Paired by line (donor line L = stage-out line L + 1, the provenance line): the two lines are the same text with the numerals masked, and the two values at the same position name the same symbol + offset, each in its own image (twin symtabs).
- **DS-AB1W-LABEL** (3 rows; DIFFERENT-SYMBOL; TOOL-LIMIT): AB1w rows whose name is a label carried from cgif_file_fuzzer's AB1w by the released donor, not the symbol: tfib_A_strlen .. tfib_A_wctomb hold the 4-byte wrappers __wrap_<f> = __interceptor_trampoline_<f> the program calls, tfib_A_asan_register_globals holds __asan_register_elf_globals, and so on. The dst value is the same symbol + offset (A) or the same end (E) in fuzz_transport as the src value in fuzz_grant. The named-symbol rule reads the label as a symbol name and expects the label-named symbol.
- **PATCH-DEADARM** (3 rows; UNPAIRED-SOURCE; OK-explained): dead-arm-ad1g.patch: the arm `|| (change SPAN with (1482 * 4096); apply Z_mod_mult)` of A/AD1g.v is dead in the donor (1482 * 4096 != SPAN) and removed in the port; the deleted arm's numerals have no partner. The owner's decision.
- **SDCP-SIZE** (3 rows; STALE-DONOR-CODE-POINTER; TOOL-LIMIT): 262144 = 0x40000 in a register value of the allocator's free-array mapping walk: the mmap length (x11) and the register the code fills with `lui xN, 0x40` (PopulateFreeArray, EnsureFreeArraySpace), at the same function offsets in both images. A size; 0d24592683b6's change (2) reads it as a code pointer because 0x40000 lies inside a function of fuzz_grant.
- **K-TLS** (2 rows; KIND-CHANGED; OK-explained): tls.lo = PT_TLS p_vaddr: src 0x9b168 (.text.hi 0x9b164 + pad), dst 0xa2948. In dst .text.hi = tls.lo = load1.memhi = 0xa2948, so the checker reads the value as an image extent.
- **P-SMCLASS** (2 rows; UNPAIRED-PORTED; OK-explained): AB1w rows of __asan_stack_malloc_5: fuzz_transport links classes 0/1/2/4/5, fuzz_grant 0/1/2/4.
- **N-TOT** (2 rows; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): A/AFem.v TOT bound: TOT = length (ly_tr0 ly) + 1 + depot_frames S <= 31 + 1 + 31 * DEPOT_MAX 4096 = 127008, a count (override rows of overrides.tsv; the owner's decision). 2 of the 2 dst values are override rows of `overrides.tsv` (each equal to its new value).
- **N-IAL** (2 rows; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): witness of (P + 8k) mod 8 = 0 with P = TFIB_IAL_LO = internal_alloc_placeholder: dst 0xadc00 / 8 = 88960; src 0xa5d40 / 8 = 84904. TFIB_IAL_LO is the start-up LIB/A/AF0m.v's.
- **N-CELLPATCH** (2 rows; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): cell-lookup.patch: `apply (nth_error_In _ 60)` (60 = list index) is replaced by `apply (AF0x.cell_lookup_In _ (ANum (TFIB_OOM + 1)))`; the checker pairs the index 60 with the byte offset 1. Neither is an address.
- **U-TLSSH** (2 rows; UNRESOLVED; OK-explained): 0x7fff8000 + a/8 with a = builtin_tls+0xeb8 in both (builtin_tls src 0x5d7a08, dst 0x5f3a28, size 0x200d0 both); a lower bound on SMv that stays below shadow(IMG_TOP) in both trees.
- **SV-HWCAP** (2 rows; SAME-VALUE-DIFFERENT-OBJECT; TOOL-LIMIT): KRange (EVar V_HW) 0x112D 0x20112D: the range of the AT_HWCAP value; 0x112D = ISA bits a, c, d, f, i, m; 0x20112D adds bit 21 (v). Not an address; the checker reads it as theDepot+off.
- **GF-FMTPTR** (2 rows; GENERATOR-FILL; OK-explained): GENERATOR-FILL row: the same string + offset in both images (as N-FMTPTR), and the donor line holds the same value as often as the dst line, so the duplicate is the donor's.
- **U-RODATA-HI** (1 row; UNRESOLVED; OK-explained): A/AB1w.v tfib_rodata_hi: the donor's value is a jump-table cell 0x40 below fuzz_grant's .rodata end; port_core 83f5203d2a2f writes fuzz_transport's .rodata end 0x22b4c (section header), the owner's decision.
- **U-PHDR** (1 row; UNRESOLVED; OK-explained): AT_PHDR value: PT_PHDR p_vaddr = 0x10040 in both images.
- **N-AD1o-TOP** (1 row; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): (IMG_TOP - 1) / 8: dst (0x616000 - 1) / 8 = 797695 (src (0x5fa000 - 1) / 8 = 783359); the start-up LIB/A/AB13.v TFIB_BSS gives the same top.
- **P-AF0L** (1 row; UNPAIRED-PORTED; OK-explained): AF0l Tfib_code pc_stack_malloc: fuzz_transport has classes 0/1/2/4/5, fuzz_grant 0/1/2/4; the port wrote fuzz_transport's own class map, so the added arm has no partner.
- **N-DEPOT** (1 row; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): witness TFIB_DEPOT / 4: dst _ZN11__sanitizerL8theDepotE = 0x18e8d8, / 4 = 408118; src 0x186a18 / 4 = 400006. TFIB_DEPOT is the start-up LIB/A/AF0m.v's.
- **N-INST** (1 row; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): (TFIB_INST + 168) / 8 with TFIB_INST = __asan::instance: dst (0xa9398 + 168) / 8 = 86664; src (0xa14d8 + 168) / 8 = 82608. TFIB_INST is the start-up LIB/A/AF0m.v's.
- **N-IMGSPAN** (1 row; NON-ADDRESS-CHANGED; OK-explained): the image span IMG_TOP - IMG_LO: dst 0x606000 = 0x616000 - 0x10000, src 0x5ea000 = 0x5fa000 - 0x10000, on a line that adds it to IMG_LO (`0x10000 + SPAN`, the image top; A/AD1g.v static_img_chk). 205a90fd19b8 flags it in its size-name slot rule; it is an image size that moves with the image.
- **UM-AB1W-LABEL** (1 row; UNMEASURED-NAME; OK-explained): UNMEASURED-NAME (reported, measured by nothing): as DS-AB1W-LABEL, rows whose label names a symbol of another spelling (gmtime_r / __gmtime_r ...); the dst value is the same symbol + offset as the src value.
